     1	# G85 exact derivation — mixed time-live completion archetypes
     2	
     3	## 1. Result first
     4	
     5	The G84 mixed-branch obstruction is not a generic obstruction to a smooth complete metric. It is
     6	an obstruction to retaining too few complete-metric channels at the candidate seam.
     7	
     8	For every one of the `196` nonzero-mixing AM controls:
     9	
    10	- retaining only the stationary mixing, or making only that mixing time-dependent, remains
    11	  degenerate at the equatorial axial fixed set;
    12	- a smooth nonvanishing clock-radial shift gives a regular Lorentzian metric on the declared
    13	  `R x S^3` candidate while preserving the exact G75 north cell;
    14	- a smooth negative clock-norm lift also gives a regular Lorentzian metric but removes the
    15	  stationary lapse-zero asymptote; and
    16	- tapering the angular mixing at least as fast as the lapse gives a smooth uniformly null seam
    17	  when the remaining zero-shift coefficient is smooth in the regular completion chart; a separate
    18	  shift-supported construction gives a global time-live realization.
    19	
    20	These are mutually distinct kinematic completions. The current metric identities select none of
    21	them. The exact landing is
    22	
    23	```text
    24	COMPLETE_METRIC_CHANNELS_ADMIT_MULTIPLE_KINEMATIC_COMPLETION_CLASSES
    25	__NO_NATIVE_HISTORY_SELECTED.
    26	```
    27	
    28	## 2. General seam algebra
    29	
    30	On the G84 minimal doubled spatial candidate, consider the complete-metric slice
    31	
    32	```text
    33	ds^2/R^2 = u dτ^2 +2b dτdχ +4dχ^2
    34	           +D dθ^2+C dψ^2+2H dτdψ,
    35	D=4sin(χ)^2,
    36	C=D sin(θ)^2,
    37	H=h sin(θ)^2.
    38	```
    39	
    40	The frozen stationary continuation is `u=-cos(chi)^2`, `b=0`, and
    41	`h=4sin(chi)^2 q(4sin(chi)^2)`. The spatial `chi,theta,psi` block is positive away from ordinary
    42	polar-coordinate fixed sets. Direct expansion gives
    43	
    44	```text
    45	det(g/R^2) = D[(4u-b^2)C-4H^2],                    (1)
    46	
    47	S_time = u-b^2/4-H^2/C.                            (2)
    48	```
    49	
    50	At either axial fixed point, the axial one-form vanishes. The invariant clock-radial block is
    51	
    52	```text
    53	G_H = [[u_H,b_H],[b_H,4]],
    54	det G_H = 4u_H-b_H^2.                              (3)
    55	```
    56	
    57	Since the radial direction is positive, the local regular-Lorentz condition is
    58	
    59	```text
    60	4u_H-b_H^2 < 0.                                    (4)
    61	```
    62	
    63	Equation (4) is pointwise. Time derivatives of `h` cannot change it. This is why turning on time
    64	without turning on another metric channel does not repair G84.
    65	
    66	## 3. Causal type of the candidate seam
    67	
    68	The induced metric on `chi=pi/2` has determinant
    69	
    70	```text
    71	det(g_seam/R^2) = D(u_H C-H^2).                     (5)
    72	```
    73	
    74	For the lapse-zero classes `u_H=0`:
    75	
    76	- if `h_H!=0`, equation (5) is negative off axis, so the seam is timelike there; at the axial
    77	  fixed set `H=0`, it becomes null;
    78	- if `h_H=0`, the induced metric is degenerate at every angular point, so a regular ambient
    79	  completion has a uniformly null seam.
    80	
    81	Thus nonzero angular mixing and a uniform horizon-like seam are incompatible within this declared
    82	metric block at `u_H=0`. A radial shift repairs the ambient metric but does not change the induced
    83	seam metric, so it does not make the nonzero-mixing seam uniformly null.
    84	
    85	If `u_H<0`, equation (5) is negative everywhere after regular angular coordinates are used. The
    86	seam is timelike, and the stationary lapse-zero asymptote has been removed.
    87	
    88	## 4. Constructive time-live global witnesses
    89	
    90	Let `chi_0=pi/6`, the exact edge of the inherited G75 north cell, and choose
    91	`chi_1` with `chi_0<chi_1<pi/2`. A `C-infinity` gate can be made from
    92	
    93	```text
    94	eta(y)=0                    for y<=0,
    95	eta(y)=exp(-1/y)            for y>0,
    96	S(y)=eta(y)/(eta(y)+eta(1-y)),
    97	W(chi)=S((chi-chi_0)/(chi_1-chi_0))
    98	       S(((pi-chi)-chi_0)/(chi_1-chi_0)).           (6)
    99	```
   100	
   101	Then `W=0` on the registered north cell and near the south pole, while `W=1` in a band containing
   102	the equator. The numerical verifier uses the corresponding `C2` quintic gate; equation (6) proves
   103	that no finite differentiability compromise is needed for existence.
   104	
   105	### 4.1 Clock-radial shift family
   106	
   107	For arbitrary `B>0`, `|epsilon|<1`, and real `omega`, set
   108	
   109	```text
   110	b(τ,chi)=B W(chi)[1+epsilon sin(omega τ)],
   111	u=-cos(chi)^2.                                      (7)
   112	```
   113	
   114	At the seam, `b_H` never vanishes and equation (4) is strictly negative. Away from the seam,
   115	`u<0` already gives the correct signature. Near the poles `W=0`; the mixing one-form is smooth
   116	because
   117	
   118	```text
   119	h sin(theta)^2 dψ=q(r^2)(X dY-Y dX),                (8)
   120	```
   121	
   122	with `r=2sin(chi)`. Equations (6)--(8) therefore define a smooth, time-live Lorentz metric on the
   123	declared `R x S^3` candidate for every frozen polynomial `q`. It equals the G75 control exactly on
   124	its authoritative cell.
   125	
   126	The condition `b_H(t)!=0` is load-bearing. If it crosses zero while `u_H=0`, the axial degeneracy
   127	returns at that time.
   128	
   129	### 4.2 Clock-norm lift family
   130	
   131	For arbitrary `L>0`, `|epsilon|<1`, and real `omega`, set
   132	
   133	```text
   134	u=-cos(chi)^2-L W(chi)[1+epsilon sin(omega τ)],
   135	b=0.                                                (9)
   136	```
   137	
   138	Now `u_H<0`, so equations (2)--(5) give a smooth Lorentz metric and a timelike seam. This family
   139	also preserves the inherited north cell. It does not preserve the candidate lapse-zero asymptote;
   140	that is a classification, not a defect.
   141	
   142	### 4.3 Mixing-taper families
   143	
   144	Because continuation beyond `chi_0` is free, define a smooth continuation equal to the frozen
   145	mixing on the inherited cell and satisfying
   146	
   147	```text
   148	h=A h_tilde,   A=cos(chi)^2                         (10)
   149	```
   150	
   151	near the seam, with `h_tilde` smooth in regular `U,V` completion coordinates. The stationary
   152	subcase `h_tilde=h_tilde(A,angles)` is included. Arbitrary dependence on the singular static time
   153	`tau=log(V/U)` is not automatically smooth. A stronger explicit witness sets `h=0` on an
   154	equatorial band by using (6).
   155	
   156	For the zero-shift bifurcate chart, use the G84 coordinates
   157	
   158	```text
   159	U=2cos(chi)e^(-τ/2),
   160	V=2cos(chi)e^(+τ/2),
   161	A=UV/4,
   162	dτ=dV/V-dU/U.
   163	```
   164	
   165	Then
   166	
   167	```text
   168	h dτ=(h_tilde/4)(U dV-V dU),                        (11)
   169	```
   170	
   171	which is smooth under the stated regular-coordinate condition. Equation (10), not merely `h_H=0`,
   172	is the sufficient zero-shift rate condition; it is not permission for arbitrary static-time
   173	dependence.
   174	Alternatively, combine the taper with (7); the ambient metric is globally regular in the
   175	`R x S^3` chart and equation (5) gives a uniformly null seam.
   176	
   177	## 5. Full frozen census
   178	
   179	The production and independent implementations parse the original rational polynomials rather
   180	than trusting the G84 count. They reproduce:
   181	
   182	```text
   183	196 mixed profiles,
   184	104 q(4)>0,
   185	 92 q(4)<0.
   186	```
   187	
   188	Their inherited behavior classes are `24` center-off, `20` endpoint-tapered, `36` sign-changing,
   189	`112` persistent-sign, and `4` zero-at-both-original-boundaries. Each appears under all five
   190	archetypes, giving exactly `980` unique rows.
   191	
   192	The completion result is independent of the sign and magnitude of `q(4)` because the mixing enters
   193	the Schur complement as a negative square. This is an algebraic classification, not evidence that
   194	all profiles are physically equivalent.
   195	
   196	## 6. What this does and does not close
   197	
   198	G85 closes the narrow question left by G84: nonzero mixing is not globally dead. It can coexist
   199	with a smooth complete metric once other complete-coframe channels are allowed. It also sharpens
   200	the candidate asymptote question: keeping nonzero seam mixing prevents `chi=pi/2` from being one
   201	uniform null observer wall in this class.
   202	
   203	But the locally jet-open complete coframe admits all these histories. Maurer--Cartan compatibility
   204	does not choose among them, and no native evolution/global-selection law is supplied. Therefore
   205	G85 derives neither the physical continuation nor physical `X_max`.
   206	
   207	No profile, topology, `R`, endpoint, CMB source or observable, action, matter law, bootstrap
   208	closure, boundary functional, or signalling interpretation is selected.

    30	    )
    31	    assert process.returncode == 0, process.stdout + process.stderr

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Independent G85 verifier; does not import the production derivation."""

     9	import math
    10	from collections import Counter

    24	    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

    32	def verify_manifest() -> None:
    33	    rows = table(HERE / "SOURCE_MANIFEST.tsv")
    34	    assert len(rows) == len({row["path"] for row in rows}) == 11
    35	    assert all((ROOT / row["path"]).is_file() and sha(ROOT / row["path"]) == row["sha256"] for row in rows)

    37	
    38	def independent_profile_census() -> tuple[dict[str, str], Counter[str], Counter[str]]:
    39	    s = sp.symbols("s", real=True)
    40	    source = {row["profile_id"]: row for row in table(G75) if row["lapse_name"] == "AM"}
    41	    frozen = table(G84)
    42	    assert len(source) == len(frozen) == 197
    43	    q4: dict[str, str] = {}
    44	    signs: Counter[str] = Counter()
    45	    behaviors: Counter[str] = Counter()
    46	    for row in frozen:
    47	        if row["profile_id"] == "G75_F01_AM":
    48	            continue
    49	        src = source[row["profile_id"]]
    50	        expr = sp.Poly(sp.sympify(src["q_of_s"], locals={"s": s}), s).as_expr()
    51	        value = sp.factor(expr.subs(s, 4))
    52	        assert value != 0
    53	        assert sp.simplify(value - sp.sympify(row["q_at_s_4_exact"])) == 0
    54	        q4[row["profile_id"]] = sp.sstr(value)
    55	        signs["positive" if value > 0 else "negative"] += 1
    56	        behaviors[src["behavior_class"]] += 1
    57	    assert len(q4) == 196
    58	    return q4, signs, behaviors
    59	
    60	
    61	def independent_algebra() -> dict[str, str]:
    62	    # Direct cofactor expansion, deliberately not a Matrix.det replay of production.
    63	    u, b, h, D, z = sp.symbols("u b h D z", real=True)
    64	    C = D * z
    65	    H = h * z
    66	    three_by_three = sp.expand(4 * u * C - b * b * C - 4 * H * H)
    67	    determinant = sp.factor(D * three_by_three)
    68	    schur = sp.factor(u - b * b / 4 - H * H / C)
    69	    induced = sp.factor(D * (u * C - H * H))
    70	    assert determinant == -D * z * (D * b**2 - 4 * D * u + 4 * h**2 * z)
    71	    assert sp.simplify(schur + (D * b**2 - 4 * D * u + 4 * h**2 * z) / (4 * D)) == 0
    72	    assert sp.simplify(induced + D * z * (-D * u + h**2 * z)) == 0
    73	    return {
    74	        "determinant": sp.sstr(determinant),
    75	        "schur": sp.sstr(schur),
    76	        "induced": sp.sstr(induced),
    77	        "axis_gate": sp.sstr(4 * u - b**2),
    78	    }
    79	
    80	
    81	def smootherstep(y: float) -> float:
    82	    if y <= 0:
    83	        return 0.0
    84	    if y >= 1:
    85	        return 1.0
    86	    return 6 * y**5 - 15 * y**4 + 10 * y**3
    87	
    88	
    89	def gate(chi: float) -> float:
    90	    edge = math.pi / 6
    91	    plateau = math.pi / 3
    92	    north = smootherstep((chi - edge) / (plateau - edge))
    93	    south = smootherstep(((math.pi - chi) - edge) / (plateau - edge))
    94	    return north * south
    95	
    96	
    97	def numeric_signature_witnesses(q4: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, float | int]:
    98	    # Seam and neighboring points; amplitudes of h cannot spoil Lorentz signature because
    99	    # they enter the temporal Schur complement with a negative square.
   100	    min_shift_margin = math.inf
   101	    min_lift_margin = math.inf
   102	    checked = 0
   103	    for tau in np.linspace(-math.pi, math.pi, 17):
   104	        time_factor = 1.0 + 0.25 * math.sin(tau)
   105	        for chi in np.linspace(math.pi / 6, 5 * math.pi / 6, 33):
   106	            A = math.cos(chi) ** 2
   107	            w = gate(chi)
   108	            b = w * time_factor
   109	            u_shift = -A
   110	            u_lift = -A - w * time_factor
   111	            shift_margin = b * b - 4 * u_shift
   112	            lift_margin = -4 * u_lift
   113	            if abs(chi - math.pi / 2) < 1e-12:
   114	                min_shift_margin = min(min_shift_margin, shift_margin)
   115	                min_lift_margin = min(min_lift_margin, lift_margin)
   116	            assert shift_margin >= -1e-14
   117	            assert lift_margin >= -1e-14
   118	            checked += 1
   119	    assert min_shift_margin >= 0.75**2
   120	    assert min_lift_margin >= 3.0
   121	    assert gate(math.pi / 6) == 0.0 and gate(math.pi / 2) == 1.0 and gate(5 * math.pi / 6) == 0.0
   122	    assert len(q4) == 196
   123	    return {
   124	        "grid_checks": checked,
   125	        "minimum_shift_axis_margin_at_seam": min_shift_margin,
   126	        "minimum_lift_axis_margin_at_seam": min_lift_margin,
   127	        "profiles_covered_by_symbolic_square_argument": len(q4),
   128	    }
   129	
   130	
   131	def kruskal_taper_check() -> dict[str, str]:
   132	    U, V, htilde = sp.symbols("U V htilde", real=True)
   133	    A = U * V / 4
   134	    # h=A*htilde and dτ=dV/V-dU/U.
   135	    coeff_dv = sp.simplify(A * htilde / V)
   136	    coeff_du = sp.simplify(-A * htilde / U)
   137	    assert coeff_dv == U * htilde / 4
   138	    assert coeff_du == -V * htilde / 4
   139	    return {
   140	        "coefficient_dV": sp.sstr(coeff_dv),
   141	        "coefficient_dU": sp.sstr(coeff_du),
   142	        "required_coordinate_home": "HTILDE_SMOOTH_IN_REGULAR_U_V_CHART",
   143	        "singular_static_tau_dependence_automatically_allowed": "false",
   144	        "result": "SMOOTH_WHEN_HTILDE_IS_KRUSKAL_SMOOTH",
   145	    }
   146

 succeeded in 0ms:
   110	```text
   111	A(s)=cos[s/(2R)]^2,       0<=s<πR.
   112	```
   113	
   114	With the receiver normalized at the patch center, the G79/G83 Killing-observer depth and terminal
   115	reciprocal readout become
   116	
   117	```text
   118	φ(s) = -log cos[s/(2R)],
   119	c_eff(s)/c_E = exp[-2φ(s)] = cos[s/(2R)]^2.
   120	```
   121	
   122	Therefore
   123	
   124	```text
   125	s -> πR from below  =>  φ -> +infinity,
   126	                         c_eff/c_E -> 0.
   127	```
   128	
   129	Every central geodesic observer in the isometry orbit receives the same stationary-patch limit
   130	`πR`. This is the first exact frame-shared recentered asymptote in the current branch record.
   131	Its exact status is
   132	
   133	```text
   134	DERIVED_CONDITIONAL_ZERO_MIXING_CONSTANT_CURVATURE_GEODESIC_OBSERVER_CLASS.
   135	```
   136	
   137	It is not yet physical `X_max`: zero mixing is one control, `R` is unselected, arbitrary
   138	accelerated observers are not covered, and the physical pair-separation operator has not been
   139	identified with this static-patch distance.
   140	
   141	## 5. Why stationary depth is not global spatial distance
   142	
   143	The completed spatial sphere and the observer-patch law are related but not identical:
   144	
   145	1. North pole to equator has `d_S3=πR` and divergent stationary depth.
   146	2. Entirely within the north static patch, take two observers at `χ=π/4` separated by angular
   147	   angle `γ`. They have identical lapse and hence zero endpoint lapse-depth, while
   148	   `d_S3/R=2 arccos[(1+cosγ)/2]` ranges continuously from zero to `π`. This avoids treating the
   149	   opposite static patch as one future-directed stationary pair query.
   150	3. Pairs lying within the equatorial two-sphere can have spatial distances ranging from zero to
   151	   `2πR`, while the original stationary observer congruence becomes null there.
   152	
   153	Thus no single-valued function `φ=f(d_S3)` exists even within one regular static patch of the
   154	minimal completed spatial sphere. The candidate
   155	`πR` is an observer-patch accessibility scale, not the global spatial diameter. If it becomes
   156	physical `X_max`, then the missing UDT separation type cannot be unrestricted spatial geodesic
   157	distance on the completed slice.
   158	
   159	This resolves G83's receiver-dependent one-sided lengths only in the central-geodesic recentered
   160	class. A receiver held at fixed nonzero radius is accelerated and is not silently promoted into
   161	that class.
   162	
   163	## 6. Complete nonzero-mixing continuation
   164	
   165	In `χ`, the complete continued metric is
   166	
   167	```text
   168	ds^2/R^2 = -cos(χ)^2 dτ^2
   169	            +4[dχ^2+sin(χ)^2dΩ^2]
   170	            +2h(χ)sin(θ)^2dτdψ,
   171	h(χ)=4sin(χ)^2 q(4sin(χ)^2).
   172	```
   173	
   174	At the equator, the `τ-ψ` block has determinant
   175	
   176	```text
   177	-h(π/2)^2 sin(θ)^4.
   178	```
   179	
   180	It is Lorentzian away from the axial fixed points when `h(π/2)!=0`. At either axial fixed point,
   181	the axial Killing vector vanishes, the mixing one-form vanishes, and `g(∂τ,·)=0`; the analytic
   182	`χ` continuation is genuinely degenerate there. A corotating redefinition cannot remove this
   183	fixed-point kernel because adding the vanishing axial generator leaves `∂τ` unchanged on the axis.
   184	
   185	Direct exact evaluation of every frozen AM polynomial at `s=x^2=4` gives:
   186	
   187	```text
   188	q(4)=0:     1 row, the zero-mixing control;
   189	q(4)!=0: 196 rows.
   190	```
   191	
   192	Hence all 196 nonzero-mixing continuations are obstructed within this standard smooth,
   193	stationary, axial, doubled/bifurcate extension class. This is not a generic no-go. A different
   194	time-live completion, symmetry reduction, branch topology, or metric evolution could change the
   195	extension problem, and none is supplied here.
   196

**Verdict**
`VERIFIED_WITH_CAVEATS`.

The core G85 landing survives within the sealed intake and its declared scope: I found no new scientific correction, no hidden topology/profile/scale selection, and no illicit promotion to physical `X_max` or native dynamics. I verified `36/36` payload SHA-256 entries against [REVIEW_MANIFEST.tsv](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/REVIEW_MANIFEST.tsv:1), and computed `sha256(REVIEW_MANIFEST.tsv)=f8443b0c2032730af68bccca6a528f0714cf012ce36fcf952f705ff688a9d507`.

**Corrections**
- No new scientific correction is required.
- The only load-bearing precision correction remains the one already embedded in the intake: the zero-shift taper condition is not merely `h_H=0`; it is `h=A*h_tilde` with `A=cos(chi)^2=UV/4` and `h_tilde` smooth in regular `U,V` coordinates. Stronger-than-`A` order is not justified by the sealed algebra. See [EXACT_DERIVATION.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/EXACT_DERIVATION.md:147), [REVIEW_MANIFEST_CORRECTION.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/REVIEW_MANIFEST_CORRECTION.md:3), and [RUN_RECORD.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/RUN_RECORD.md:36).

**Independent recomputation**
- With `D=4 sin^2 chi`, `z=sin^2 theta`, `C=D z`, `H=h z`, I recomputed
  `det(g/R^2)=D[(4u-b^2)C-4H^2]`,
  `S_time=u-b^2/4-H^2/C`,
  `det G_H=4u_H-b_H^2`,
  `det(g_seam/R^2)=D(u_H C-H^2)`. See [EXACT_DERIVATION.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/EXACT_DERIVATION.md:44).
- From that algebra: `h`-only time dependence cannot repair the axial degeneracy because `H` vanishes on the axis and the gate is pointwise `4u_H-b_H^2<0`; with `u_H=0,h_H!=0` the seam is timelike off-axis and null on the axial subset; with `u_H<0` it is timelike everywhere in regular angular coordinates; and `h=A*h_tilde` gives `h dτ=(h_tilde/4)(U dV-V dU)`. See [EXACT_DERIVATION.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/EXACT_DERIVATION.md:57) and [EXACT_DERIVATION.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/EXACT_DERIVATION.md:156).
- The shift/lift witnesses preserve the exact G75 north cell because the gate `W(chi)` is zero on `chi<=pi/6`; the shift term is shut off near poles, and the mixing one-form is regular in Cartesian form. See [EXACT_DERIVATION.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/EXACT_DERIVATION.md:90).
- Raw frozen-source counts recomputed from G75/G84: `197` AM rows total, `1` zero-mixing control, `196` mixed rows, sign counts `104 positive / 92 negative`, behavior counts `24 / 20 / 36 / 112 / 4`, and `196 x 5 = 980` unique profile/archetype rows. Saved atlas counts are `392 POINTWISE_DEGENERATE`, `196 CONDITIONAL_ON_NONVANISHING_SHIFT`, `196 REGULAR_LORENTZ_NONNULL_SEAM`, `196 REGULAR_LORENTZ_UNIFORM_NULL_SEAM`. See [G84 EXACT_DERIVATION.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G84_am_global_completion_pair_diameter_audit_2026-08-12/EXACT_DERIVATION.md:185), [PREREGISTRATION.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/PREREGISTRATION.md:43), and [AUDIT_REPORT.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/AUDIT_REPORT.md:11).

**Caveats**
- “Complete metric” here is not a proof of geodesic completeness; [COMPLETENESS_SCOPE.md](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/COMPLETENESS_SCOPE.md:11) explicitly says geodesic completeness is not proved.
- The independent verifier is implementation-independent, not clean-room independent: it shares the same frozen data and symbolic stack, and part of it rechecks saved artifacts after recomputing the algebra/census. See [verify_independent.py](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/verify_independent.py:61) and [verify_independent.py](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/verify_independent.py:148).
- The catch suite is regression evidence, not standalone proof, and the package’s preregistration-preservation / repository-gate claims depend on live git/repository state outside the sealed intake boundary. See [run_catch_proofs.py](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/run_catch_proofs.py:26) and [verify_package.py](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/udt_cmb_G85_mixed_timelive_completion_atlas_2026-08-12/verify_package.py:56).

**Reproducibility**
- `CORE_REPRODUCIBLE_WITHIN_SEALED_INTAKE`: yes; I independently replayed the determinant/Schur/seam algebra, the Kruskal taper identity, and the full mixed-profile census from the sealed files.
- `PACKAGED_CLI_RERUN_HERE`: no; this review sandbox is read-only and the packaged scripts overwrite artifacts.
- `PREREG_GIT_AND_REPOSITORY_GATES_WITHIN_BOUNDARY`: not independently replayed.

**Evidence Boundary**
- Exact boundary: [REVIEW_MANIFEST.tsv](/tmp/udt_g85_review_rySBMpzp/REVIEW_MANIFEST.tsv:1) plus its 36 listed payload files only.
